West of House
You are standing in an open field west of a white house, with a boarded front door.
There is a small mailbox here.


Starting location: West House
Target score: 80
Model: openai/gpt-4o-mini | Agent type: smart
Maximum steps: 500

LESSONS LEARNED FROM PREVIOUS EPISODES:
Repeat these positive behaviors:
1. Behind House: 'open window' then 'west' recorded +10 at the Kitchen. Do this early and directly, without wandering the forest.
2. Living Room: open the trap door and go 'down' for +25 at the Cellar. Carry the lantern, lit, before going.
3. Up a Tree 'take egg' gave +5 and Living Room 'put egg in case' +5. Bank treasures promptly after collecting them.
4. Gallery 'take art' gave +4, and 'put painting in case' in the Living Room gave +6. Carry the art straight home.
5. After banking, if about 20 moves pass with no score, mark the goal stale and try an unvisited exit, such as the Attic or Forest paths.
Avoid these negative behaviors:
1. Treat the Maze 'take coins' goal as unreachable. It was reissued about 15 times, never reached, and ignoring it cost nothing.
2. Don't shuttle among Cellar, East Chasm, Gallery and Studio. About 370 steps after step 35 scored nothing. Cap revisits to a room at two.
3. Never drop the lantern. Dropping it in East Chasm and then moving north in the dark caused the grue death (-10). In darkness, don't move or fiddle.
4. Avoid filler actions: repeated open/close sack, taking and dropping the manual, garlic and lunch. None of it scored.
5. Don't go north from the Cellar toward the Troll Room. An earlier run died there and this run's notes call the route blocked.
Apply these lessons when relevant; current game feedback takes precedence.

You are about 10 feet above the ground nestled among some large branches. The nearest branch above you is above your reach.

The egg appears extremely fragile.

You are at the top of the Great Canyon on its west wall. From here there is a marvelous view of the canyon and parts of the Frigid River upstream. Across the canyon, the walls of the White Cliffs join the mighty ramparts of the Flathead Mountains to the east. Historian episode review — episode 28
================================================

Review source: llm
Lessons: 5 positive + 5 negative (configured per category: 5)

The recorded evidence for this episode does not match the supplied summary. It shows 20 points over 500 steps and a step-limit end. The agent never went underground, there was no grue death, and no Cellar, art or case events appear in it. Confirmed Actor strengths: taking the egg (+5), the Kitchen entry (+10), and the first deposit in the case (+5). The Actor then wasted most of the run. It took the egg back out of the case (-5) and redeposited it (+5) twice, for no net gain. It looped through Forest, Clearing, Forest Path and Up a Tree, and shuttled Kitchen/Living Room/Attic, with window open/close filler. It also never tried an unexplored frontier. Navigation was clean, with every move confirmed. The symbolic layer reissued the failed Maze 'take coins' goal about 13 times, and the Actor kept following it. Next episode: ignore that goal, deposit once, then explore a new frontier with light.

LESSONS LEARNED
================================
POSITIVES TO REPEAT
1. Behind House with window open: 'west' into the Kitchen recorded +10 once. Don't expect repeat points from re-entering; look for new sources afterward.
2. Up a Tree 'take egg' gave +5 and Living Room 'put egg in case' +5. Bank a treasure promptly, then leave it in the case.
3. An earlier run recorded +25 going 'down' through the Living Room trap door to the Cellar. Unverified this episode; carry a lit lantern if retrying.
4. In dark rooms like the Attic, retreat at once. No grue death occurred this run, so keep that discipline.
5. After about 20 moves without score, commit to an unvisited exit or item rather than revisiting known rooms, and compare score before and after each action.

NEGATIVES TO AVOID
1. Mark Maze 'take coins' stale after one failed attempt. It was reissued about 13 times this run with zero progress and about 250 wasted steps.
2. Avoid 2-cycles such as Kitchen↔Living Room, Kitchen↔Behind House and Clearing↔Forest Path. Visit a room at most twice without a new score event.
3. Never take the egg back out of the trophy case. It cost -5, the redeposit gave +5, and it was done twice for net zero.
4. Skip filler: open/close window, Attic in-and-out, and 'go around forest/trees' loops through Forest rooms #76-78. None scored.
5. Never drop the lantern; an earlier run died to a grue (-10) after moving north in the dark. In darkness, don't wander or fiddle.
